# Graph Report - polymath_v3.3  (2026-08-04)

## Corpus Check
- 4595 files · ~11,141,383 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 29569 nodes · 55034 edges · 1748 communities (1674 shown, 74 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 84% EXTRACTED · 16% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 8995 edges (avg confidence: 0.72)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `86149227`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
